Let's design a robust optimization for m=64/128:
1. Parametrize x on simplex (sum=1, x>=0). Score =2m * max c.
2. Use smooth surrogate: F_β(x)= (2m) * (logsumexp(β c_k) / β) maybe. Minimizing this approximates max and is smooth. We can use projected gradient (onto simplex) or Dirichlet parameterization (log softmax).
3. Compute c = conv(x,x) via np.convolve (or FFT). For m up to 256, direct O(m^2) fine.
4. Gradient of F_β wrt x_i: c_k = ∑_{u+v=k} x_u x_v. ∂c_k/∂x_i = 2∑_{u+v=k} [u=i x_v + v=i x_u] but since summing both, if i and k-i distinct counted twice? Let's derive: c_k = ∑_{u=0}^{m-1} x_u x_{k-u} over valid. Differentiate: ∂c_k/∂x_i = x_{k-i} (if valid) + x_{k-i} (if k-i valid? Actually term for u=i gives x_i x_{k-i}; term for u=k-i gives x_{k-i} x_i if k-i≠i; both contribute, so 2x_{k-i} if i and k-i both in range and i≠k-i; if i=k-i (2i=k) only one term? But the sum includes u=i once; derivative 2? Since c_i^2 term derivative 2x_i. Same formula 2x_{k-i} works if valid because if i=k-i, derivative of x_i*x_i is 2x_i. So ∂c_k=2 x_{k-i} if 0≤k-i<m else 0.
Then gradient of logsumexp: ∑ w_k (2 x_{k-i}), where w_k=exp(β c_k)/∑exp. This can be computed via convolution of weights with x? For each i, grad_i=2 ∑_k w_k x_{k-i} (valid). This is cross-correlation of w and x. Could compute via FFT or direct O(m^2). m small direct fine.
5. Projected gradient onto simplex: step in direction -grad, then project sorted O(m log m). Or use mirror descent: x_i ∝ exp(θ_i), θ updated += -lr grad, normalize. Mirror descent maintains positivity. But scoring scale invariant; softmax parametrization may help. Use η_i = initial - lr grad, x=softmax(η) (sum=1). Since objective scale invariant, x sum fixed.
6. Annealing: use stochastic smoothing: perturb x by small random (Dirichlet noise), accept if improved or with temperature. But gradient smooth surrogate may get stuck.

Let's design custom optimizer:

Representation: x length m, sum=1, x>0. Use log-parameters theta to ensure positivity; update theta via gradient. Because scale invariant, sum fixed by softmax. But softmax can make very small values; okay. Objective c=conv(x,x). For gradient of smooth F:

Let's design exact score function for micro:
```
def micro_score(x):
    x = np.asarray(x,float)
    s = x.sum()
    if s < 1e-12: return inf
    c = np.convolve(x, x, mode='full')
    return 2.0 * len(x) * c.max() / (s*s)
```
Scale invariant; we can maintain sum=1 to simplify. Values nonnegative.

Local search exact:
```
def local_search(x, rng, max_time, base_temp):
    best=x; best_score=score
    temp = ...
    while time<limit:
       cand = perturb(best)
       sc=score(cand)
       if sc<best or rng.random()<exp((best-sc)/temp): update
       temp *= cooling
    return best
```
Perturbations:
- Add small Gaussian to all entries: cand = best * exp(N(0,σ)) or cand = best + N(0,σ). Then project to simplex (clip, normalize).

Implementation details for gradient descent in micro:
Function `smooth_objective_grad(x, beta)`:
```
m = len(x)
c = np.convolve(x,x)  # length 2m-1
b = beta * c
maxb = b.max()
w = np.exp(b - maxb)
w /= w.sum()
F = 2*m*(maxb/beta + log(w.sum())/beta)  # since logsumexp
grad = np.zeros(m)
# grad_i = 2*m/beta? Wait derivative of 2m * LSE/β: 2m * ∑ w_k ∂c_k/β = 2m * ∑ w_k ∂c_k? Since LSE/β derivative = (1/β)∑ e^{βc}/Z ∂c = ∑ w ∂c.
for i in range(m):
    k = i
    val=0
    # sum over k where 0 <= k-i < m and 0 <= k <2m-1
    lo = max(0, i) # k-i >=0 -> k>=i
    hi = min(2m-1, i+m-1) # k-i <=m-1 -> k<=i+m-1
    # Need sum w[lo:hi] * x[k-i]
    # x indices 0..m-1 correspond j=k-i. j range max(0? )
    # Could loop k or use convolution of w with x.
```
We need grad_i = 2m * ∑_k w_k * 2? Wait ∂c_k/∂x_i = 2 x_{k-i} (j). So derivative F =2m * ∑ w_k * 2 x_{k-i}? Let's check F=2m * LSE/β. LSE/β gradient = ∑ w_k ∂c_k. ∂c_k=2x_j. So grad =4m ∑ w_k x_{k-i}? But if using exact G=2m M, subgradient at max is 4? Let's compute with one lag active: F≈2m c_k. Gradient c_k wrt x_i =2x_{k-i}. So grad=4m x_{k-i}. Formula 2x_{k-i} holds for all valid. Yes. So gradient of 2m c_k =4m? No 2m * 2 =4m. But in updating sum=1, constant factor affects lr. Use grad_raw = np.convolve weights with x. Efficient grad: For each i, sum over j valid k=i+j: grad[i] = 2 * ∑_{j=0}^{m-1} w_{i+j} x_j, where w_k defined for k=0..2m-2. This is cross-correlation of w with x: grad = 2 * (w ⊛ x) but specific: t[i]=∑_{j} w_{i+j} x_j. That is cross-correlation (not convolution) of w and x. Can compute via FFT or direct loops:
```
t = np.zeros(m)
for j in range(m):
    t += x[j] * w[j:j+m]
```
This is O(m^2)=65k, okay. If m up to512, 262k, okay. Use this after each c conv.
Then step: y = x - lr * t (maybe with factor). Project to simplex. But t may have large components; lr schedule.

Projected gradient with smoothing beta:
- Initialize beta maybe 10, increase to 100 over descent for sharper max.
- lr backtracking: Try step sizes e.g., 1e-3 down. Evaluate exact smooth F? Since F smooth, use line search. But exact score easier for accept/reject. For gradient descent, use F surrogate to ensure descent. We can compute F before and after. m small.
- Projected gradient: use Moreau projection onto simplex. Implement `project_simplex(v)`. Standard: find threshold via sorted.
- To avoid leaving positivity, projection maps to simplex.

Pseudo:
```
def gradient_step(x, beta, max_iter=100, lr_init=0.05):
    best=x; best_F=smooth_F(x,beta); best_score=exact_score(best)
    lr=lr_init
    for it in range(max_iter):
       c,f? F, grad = smooth_F_grad(x,beta)
       if norm(grad) small: break
       step = lr
       while step > 1e-8:
           y = x - step*grad
           z = project_simplex(y)
           Fz,_,_ = smooth_F_grad(z,beta)
           if Fz < F - 1e-12*abs(F): accept
           step *=0.5
       x=z
       if exact_score(z) < best_score: best=z...
       beta maybe increase
    return best
```
But computing smooth_F_grad multiple times per step costly but m small. Could use fixed lr no line search and accept based on exact score; but may oscillate.

Exact score for micro: Use `np.convolve`; m=256 length result 511. Fast.
```
def exact_score_micro(x):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    s = x.sum()
    if s <= 1e-12: return inf
    # to speed, if sum not 1 normalize? For local we keep sum1.
    if abs(s-1) > 1e-9:
       x = x/s
    c = np.convolve(x, x)
    return 2.0 * len(x) * float(c.max())
```
Since sum1, /s^2 omitted.
For safety, if c.max() NaN return inf.

Now, how to optimize micro robustly? Let's develop a multi-phase optimizer:

Phase A: Low-dimensional trigonometric optimization (D=16) for ~120 seconds.
- Define function `coeff_to_x(coeffs)`:
   m = global OMP_M (256)
   t = np.arange(m)/m
   z = zeros(m)
   for r in 1..R: z += A[r-1]*cos(2π r t)+B[r-1]*sin(2π r t)

```
def smooth_value_and_grad(x, beta):
    c=np.convolve(x,x)
    b=beta*c
    bmax=b.max()
    e=np.exp(b-bmax)
    w=e/e.sum()
    F = 2.0*len(x)*(bmax + np.log(e.sum()))/beta
    # grad raw: grad_i = 2 * sum_j w[i+j]*x_j
    grad = np.zeros_like(x)
    # Use direct loop over j for speed m=256
    for j in range(len(x)):
        grad += x[j] * w[j:j+len(x)]
    grad *= 2.0
    return F, grad
```
Check grad factor: derivative of LSE/β: ∑w ∂c; ∂c=2x_{...}. We multiply by 2 once? In loop, `grad += x[j] * w[i+j]` yields ∑ x_j w_{i+j}. Multiply by ? We need ∑ 2 x_j w = 2*s. Then F grad =2m * that =4m*s. We can omit constant 2m; use grad=s or 2s. For direction, constant not matter. Use `grad = 4.0*m* s`? If line search on F, need correct magnitude to choose lr. Projection onto simplex: standard.
```
def project_simplex(v):
    v=np.asarray(v,float)
    u=np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cssv=np.cumsum(u)
    rho=np.nonzero(u*(np.arange(1,len(u)+1))+1-cssv>0)[0][-1]
    theta=(cssv[rho]-1)/(rho+1.0)
    return np.maximum(v-theta,0)
```
This projects Euclidean onto sum=1 nonnegative. But if x from softmax sum1, y-x.

Line search:
```
def gradient_descent(x, beta, iters, lr0):
    best=x; best_score=exact; best_F=smooth_F
    lr=lr0
    for it:
       F,grad=Fgrad(x,beta)
       gnorm=np.linalg.norm(grad)
       if gnorm<1e-12: break
       direction=grad/gnorm
       # Try step size in [lr, lr*0.5...], where lr maybe 0.1
       step=lr
       improved=False
       while step>1e-8:
           y=project_simplex(x - step*direction)
           Fz,_=smooth(y,beta)
           if Fz < F - 1e-12:
                x=y; improved=True; break
           step*=0.5
       if not improved:
           # try smaller or break
           break
       exact update best
       # maybe increase beta gradually
    return best
```

def softmax(theta):
    z = theta - theta.max()

    return e/e.sum()

    F, gx = smooth_F_grad_x(x, CUR_BETA)

Exact local search algorithm details:
```
def random_local_search(x0, rng, time_limit, start_temp=0.05):
    best=x0.copy(); best_score=exact(best)
    temp=start_temp
    itr=0
    start_time=time.monotonic()
    while time.monotonic()<start_time+time_limit and best_score>target? maybe continue:
       x=best.copy()
       # choose perturbation
       u=rng.random()
       if u<0.4:
           # block multiplicative
           L=int(rng.integers(1, max(2, m//4+1)))
           st=int(rng.integers(0,m-L+1))
           sigma = 0.1 * min(1.0, np.sqrt(L/m))? Maybe 0.05
           x[st:st+L] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0, sigma, L))
       elif u<0.6:
           all noisy: x *= exp(normal(0,0.02?))
       elif u<0.8:
           # smooth low-frequency perturbation: generate coeffs 8, z, x*=exp(0.1*z normalized)
       else:
           # dirichlet mix: x2=rng.dirichlet(np.maximum(best,1e-6)+0.1); x=(1-0.2)*x+0.2*x2 normalize
       x=clip normalize
       sc=exact(x)
       if sc<best_score or (np.isfinite(best_score) and np.log(rng.random()) < (best_score-sc)/temp):
           best=x; best_score=sc
       # anneal
       temp *= 0.999? or schedule by elapsed
       # every 1000 iters, do gradient polish
       if itr%200==0:
           best, sc = optimize_theta(best, time_budget=min(2, time_limit remaining))
           update
    return best,best_score
```

def run_smooth_optimization(x0, beta_sequence, maxiters, time_limit):
    theta = np.log(np.maximum(x0,1e-12))
    theta -= theta.mean()
    best=x0; best_score=exact(x0)
    start=time.monotonic()
    for beta in beta_sequence:
       if time.time-left<... break
       CUR_BETA = float(beta)
       # maybe max_iter proportional to beta? 
       t_start=time.monotonic()
       try:
           res = minimize(theta_fun_grad, theta, method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, options={'maxiter':maxiters, 'ftol':1e-12, 'gtol':1e-8})
       except Exception:
           pass
       x = softmax(res.x)
       # maybe res.x has huge; normalize? softmax finite.
       sc=exact_score(x)
       if sc<best_score: best=x; best_score=sc
       theta = res.x - np.mean(res.x)
    return best,best_score
```

Let's code `exact_score` efficiently:
```
def exact_score(x):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    if x.size != CUR_MICRO_M? For local micro always 256; but can use len.
    s = float(x.sum())
    if s <= 1e-12: return float('inf')
    if abs(s-1.0) > 1e-8:
       x = x/s
    c = np.convolve(x, x, mode='full')
    m = x.size
    val = 2.0 * m * float(c.max())
    if not np.isfinite(val): return inf
    return val
```
Since sum normalized to1, divide by s^2 already? We normalized x, so score =2m max c. If input not normalized, we rescale. Good.

Let's design `perturb_x(x, rng, sigma)`:
```
def perturb_x(x, rng, sigma=0.05):
    m=len(x)
    u=rng.random()
    if u<0.5:
        L=int(rng.integers(1, max(2, m//8+1)))
        st=int(rng.integers(0, m-L+1))
        x=x.copy()
        # Gaussian with zero sum? multiplicative
        noise=rng.normal(0.0, sigma, size=L)
        x[st:st+L] *= np.exp(noise)
    elif u<0.7:
        x=x.copy()
        x *= np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sigma*0.3, m))
    elif u<0.85:
        # low-frequency noise via cosine
        R=8
        t=np.arange(m)/m
        z=np.zeros(m)
        for r in range(1,R+1):
            amp=rng.normal(0, sigma/(r+1))
            ph=rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)
            z += amp*np.cos(2*np.pi*r*t + ph)
        x=x.copy()*np.exp(z)
    else:
        alpha=np.maximum(x,1e-6)+0.1
        x2=rng.dirichlet(alpha)
        x=(1-0.3)*x+0.3*x2
    x=np.clip(x,0,None)
    s=x.sum()
    if s<=0: return x
    return x/s
```
Use sigma schedule from 0.2 down to 1e-3.

```
def local_random_search(x0, rng, time_limit):
    best=x0.copy(); best_score=exact_score(best)
    start=time.monotonic()
    temp=0.05
    noise=0.15
    no_improve=0
    while time.monotonic()-start < time_limit and time.monotonic()<DEADLINE:
       cand=perturb_x(best, rng, noise)
       sc=exact_score(cand)
       if sc < best_score:
           best=cand; best_score=sc; no_improve=0
           # sometimes smooth polish
           if no_improve? Actually after improvement, if score<target? continue
       else:
           no_improve += 1
           if rng.random() < math.exp((best_score - sc) / max(temp,1e-9)):
               best=cand; best_score=sc
       if no_improve > 1000:
           # reduce noise
           noise *=0.8; temp*=0.8; no_improve=0
       if best_score < 1.45: break
       # every 5000 iterations if time enough, polish best
    return best,best_score
```
But this is pure random; may not improve much. We can after each improvement or every N iterations run `optimize_smooth(best, [50,100], maxiter=100, time_left=1)` to polish. Use time check.

Detailed `optimize_smooth`:
```
def optimize_smooth(x0, beta_schedule=(2,10,40,120), maxiter=80, ftol=1e-12):
    theta=np.log(np.maximum(x0,1e-12)); theta-=theta.mean()
    best=x0; best_score=exact_score(x0)
    for beta in beta_schedule:
       CUR_BETA=float(beta)
       try:
          res=minimize(theta_smooth_fun_grad, theta, method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True,
                      options={'maxiter':maxiter, 'ftol':1e-12, 'gtol':1e-8, 'maxls':20})
          theta=res.x
       except Exception:
          break
       if not finite: break
       x=softmax(theta)
       sc=exact_score(x)
       if sc<best_score:
           best=x; best_score=sc
       theta=theta-np.mean(theta)
    return best,best_score
```
Set `CUR_BETA` global, but multi-thread? no.

Basin hopping with L-BFGS polishing:
```
def basin_hopping(x_best, rng, time_limit, deadline):
   best=x_best.copy(); best_score=exact_score(best)
   noise=0.1
   start=time.monotonic()
   hops=0
   while time.monotonic()<start+time_limit and time.monotonic()<deadline:
      cand=perturb_x(best, rng, noise)
      # maybe ensure cand not too similar
      # polish with beta [40,120], maxiter=50
      pol, sc = optimize_smooth(cand, (40,120), 50)
      if sc < best_score:
          best=pol; best_score=sc; hops+=1; noise=max(0.02, noise*0.95)
      else:
          # occasionally jump
          if rng.random() < 0.1:
             cand=perturb_x(best, rng, min(0.4, noise*3))
             pol,sc=optimize_smooth(cand, (20,80),50)
             if sc<best_score: ...
          noise=min(0.3, noise*1.02)
      if best_score < 1.45: break
   return best,best_score
```

def smooth_F_grad(x, beta): ...
def theta_smooth_fun_grad(theta):
    beta = CUR_BETA
    x = softmax(theta)
    F, gx = smooth_F_grad(x, beta)
    gth = x*(gx - np.dot(x,gx))
    gth += 1e-12*theta
    return F, gth
```

```
t=np.arange(m)/m
z=np.zeros(m)
for r in range(1,R+1):
    scale=1.0/(r+1)
    amp=rng.normal(0.0, scale)
    ph=rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)
    z += amp*np.cos(2*np.pi*r*t + ph)
# add overall trend
z += rng.normal(0,0.5)*(2*t-1)
f=1.0+0.8*np.tanh(z)  # nonnegative min 0.2? tanh in [-1,1], f in [0.2,1.8]
f=np.maximum(f,1e-6); return f/f.sum()
```

Basin hopping implementation needs `optimize_smooth` which uses current best. We can define top-level.

```
def basin_hopping(x0, rng, time_limit, deadline):
    best=x0.copy(); best_score=exact_score(best)
    start=time.monotonic()
    noise=0.15
    no_improve=0
    while time.monotonic() < start + time_limit and time.monotonic() < deadline - 20:
        cand = perturb_x(best, rng, noise)
        # To speed, exact score of cand; if not promising, skip polish?
        cand_score = exact_score(cand)
        if cand_score < best_score + 0.05:
            polish, sc = optimize_smooth(cand, (60,150), 80)
            if sc < best_score:
                best=polish; best_score=sc; no_improve=0; noise=max(0.03, noise*0.95)
            else:
                no_improve += 1
        else:
            no_improve += 1
        if no_improve > 50:
            noise = min(0.5, noise*1.2)
            no_improve=0
        if best_score < 1.45: break
    # final polish
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 30:
        pol,sc=optimize_smooth(best, (80,200), 100)
        if sc<best_score: best=polish...
    return best,best_score
```
This does exact score of cand cheap. Polish only if within 0.05 of best, reducing wasted L-BFGS. If no improve, increase noise. Could get stuck; initial best likely close.

Downsample helper:
```
def downsample_start(hs, m):
    hs = np.asarray(hs, dtype=float)
    hs = np.nan_to_num(hs, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    hs = np.maximum(hs,0.0)
    s=hs.sum()
    if s <=0: return None
    hs/=s
    if hs.size == m: return hs.copy()
    tt=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,m)
    xx=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,hs.size)
    out=np.interp(tt, xx, hs)
    s=out.sum()
    if s<=0: return None
    return out/s
```
If previous length 6962 uniform, downsample uniform; fine.

Let's adjust `smooth_final_F_grad` implementation for performance.

```
def smooth_final_F_grad(x, beta):
    m = x.size
    r = CUR_R
    c = np.convolve(x, x, mode='full')
    C = 2*m - 1
    c_q = c[:, None]
    c_qm1 = np.empty((C,1), dtype=float)
    c_qm1[0,0]=0.0
    c_qm1[1:,0]=c[:-1]
    s = np.arange(r, dtype=float)[None, :]
    coeff_q = s + 1.0
    coeff_qm1 = (float(r) - 1.0 - s)
    vals = c_q * coeff_q + c_qm1 * coeff_qm1
    vals[-1, -1] = -1e100  # invalid
    b = beta * vals
    bmax = float(b.max())
    e = np.exp(b - bmax)
    # invalid exp small
    ssum = float(e.sum())
    if ssum <=0 or not finite: return inf, zeros
    w = e / ssum
    F = (2.0*m / float(r)) * (bmax + math.log(ssum)) / beta
    eff_q = np.sum(w * coeff_q, axis=1)
    eff_qm1 = np.sum(w * coeff_qm1, axis=1)
    eff = eff_q.copy()
    eff[:-1] += eff_qm1[1:]
    # maybe eff has sum >0
    # compute t via convolve
    t = np.convolve(x, eff[::-1], mode='full')[2*m-2 : m-2 : -1]
    grad = (4.0*m / float(r)) * t
    return F, grad
```
Is `eff_q` using weights w (sum1 over fine lags) and coeff. Then `∂val/∂x` = 2*sum coeff x. F factor A=2m/r. grad = A*2*t =4m/r*t. Good.
Check if `vals[-1,-1]` for m=1? m=256 fine. If r=1? not.

The invalid at last q,s: Are there other invalid fine lags? Let's test q=C-1 last macro lag, s=r-1 invalid. What about q=0, s? q=0,s any 0..r-1 corresponds L=s (<r) valid; contributions c0 only for a+b=s, c_-1 zero. Valid. q=C-1,s up to r-2 valid; max L=(C-1)r+r-2=C r-2=(2m-2)r+r-2=2mr-2 valid; s=r-1 L+1 invalid. Good.

```
def _build_final_vals(c, r):
    C=len(c)
    c_q = c[:,None]
    c_qm1 = np.concatenate(([0.0], c[:-1]), axis=0)[:,None]
    s = np.arange(int(r), dtype=np.float64)[None,:]
    vals = c_q*(s+1.0) + c_qm1*(float(r)-1.0-s)
    vals[-1,-1] = -np.inf
    return vals, s, (s+1.0), (float(r)-1.0-s)
```

```
def optimize_smooth(x0, beta_schedule, maxiter):
    global CUR_BETA
    if x0 is None or len(x0)!=_MICRO_M: return x0,None
    x = np.asarray(x0,float); x=_normalize(x)
    best=x; best_score=exact_final_score(x)
    theta=np.log(np.maximum(x,1e-12)); theta -= theta.mean()
    for beta in beta_schedule:
       CUR_BETA = float(beta)
       if not HAVE_SCIPY: break
       try:
          res = minimize(theta_smooth_fun_grad, theta, method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, options={'maxiter':maxiter, 'ftol':1e-12, 'gtol':1e-6, 'maxls':50})
          if res.x is not None:
             theta = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=float)
             if not np.all(np.isfinite(theta)): break
       except Exception:
          break
       x = softmax(theta)
       sc = exact_final_score(x)
       if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
          best=x; best_score=sc
       theta = theta - np.mean(theta)
    return best, best_score
```

```
def perturb_x(x, rng, noise):
   m=x.size
   u=rng.random()
   if u<0.45:
      L=int(rng.integers(1, max(2, m//4+1)))
      st=int(rng.integers(0, m-L+1))
      out=x.copy()
      out[st:st+L] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, noise, L))
   elif u<0.7:
      out=x.copy()
      out *= np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, noise*0.3, m))
   elif u<0.9:
      out=x.copy()
      t=np.arange(m)/m
      z=np.zeros(m)
      R=8
      for r in range(1,R+1):
         amp=rng.normal(0.0, noise/(r+1))
         ph=rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)
         z += amp*np.cos(2*np.pi*r*t + ph)
      out *= np.exp(z)
   else:
      alpha=np.maximum(x,1e-6)+0.1
      x2=rng.dirichlet(alpha)
      mix=min(0.5, noise)
      out=(1.0-mix)*x + mix*x2
   out=np.maximum(out,0.0); s=out.sum(); if s<=0: return x; return out/s
```

Let's code `_harmonic_start`:
```
def _safe_positive_f(f):
    f=np.asarray(f,float)
    osc=f-1.0
    minf=f.min()
    if minf < 1e-8:
       maxneg = 1.0 - minf
       if maxneg >1e-12:
          scale=0.95/maxneg
          if scale <1.0:
             f=1.0 + scale*osc
    f=np.maximum(f,1e-8)
    return f/f.sum()
```
If f has contact zeros, scale to 1-0.95*maxneg? Actually if minf<0, maxneg=1-minf; scale=0.95/maxneg => new min=1+minf*scale =1-0.95=0.05 positive. If minf small positive, no scale. Good.

Now, if `minimize` with tuple jac requires SciPy version; if not, fallback. In code, `res = minimize(theta_smooth_fun_grad, theta, method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, ...)`. If SciPy old doesn't accept tuple with jac=True? I think it does. To be safer, we can implement alternative: define top-level `_theta_smooth_fun` and `_theta_smooth_grad` and use `jac=_theta_smooth_grad`, accepting double compute? Let's see performance. It may double function evaluations, but still okay. However, `fun` and `jac` separate may call smooth twice per L-BFGS step; with 5k evals ->10k, okay? Each eval has conv and vals; maybe 2x time but still within. Compatibility safer. But separate functions must share recent x? If called with same theta, compute twice independently. That's okay. Use:
```
def _theta_smooth_fun(theta):
    F,_=smooth_final_F_grad(softmax(theta), CUR_BETA); return F
def _theta_smooth_jac(theta):
    _,g=smooth_final_F_grad(softmax(theta), CUR_BETA); return g
```
Then `minimize(_theta_smooth_fun, theta, jac=_theta_smooth_grad, method='L-BFGS-B')`. This avoids tuple version issues. But it doubles computation. We can maybe cache last theta? Use global `_CACHE_X`, `_CACHE_F`, `_CACHE_G`, and a function to compute once with a flag? But top-level no closures; can use global variables. However, scipy may call fun and jac with same theta consecutively; we can cache to avoid recompute:
```
_CUR_THETA=None
def theta_smooth_fun(theta):
   global _CACHE
   if _CACHE is not None and np.array_equal(theta,_CACHE[0]): return _CACHE[1]
   F,g=...; _CACHE=(theta.copy(),F,g); return F
def theta_smooth_jac(theta): similar return g
```
But storing theta copy each call memory; okay. `np.allclose` maybe. Simpler not cache; double compute acceptable. m=256, 10k evals fine. Use separate for compatibility.

But separate `theta_smooth_jac` calls `smooth_final_F_grad(softmax(theta), CUR_BETA)`, which recomputes. L-BFGS might call jac after fun for same theta; double. To reduce, use tuple if available. We can try tuple inside try and fallback? Code:
```
def optimize_smooth(...):
   try:
      res = minimize(theta_smooth_fun_grad, theta, ..., jac=True)
   except TypeError:
      res = minimize(_theta_smooth_fun, theta, jac=_theta_smooth_jac, ...)
```

    s=x.sum()
    if s<=1e-300: return inf
    if abs(s-1)>1e-12: x=x/s
    m=x.size
    R=_FINAL_REPEAT
    c=np.convolve(x,x,mode='full')
    C=2*m-1
    c_q=c[:,None]

    vals[-1,-1] = -1e300
    amax = float(vals.max())
    if not np.isfinite(amax): return inf
    return 2.0*m*amax/float(R)

    x=np.asarray(unc,float)
    minv=x.min()
    if minv < 1e-6:
       # scale oscillation around 1 if possible
       if x.max() > 1.0:

def update_elite(elite, x, sc, max_len=6):
    if x is None or not np.isfinite(sc): return
    for score_ex, x_ex in elite:
        if np.linalg.norm(x - x_ex) < 1e-5 and sc >= score_ex - 1e-8:

    if len(elite)>max_len: elite=elite[:max_len]

```python
"""
Search for a short nonnegative coefficient block, then dilate it uniformly.

The evaluator's objective is scale invariant.  If a length-m sequence y is
repeated r times (with entries scaled so the final sequence has the same
sum as y or any constant scaling), the evaluator score of the final length
mr sequence is controlled by the block-wise convolution.  This program
optimizes a 256-length block directly, using a smooth log-sum-exp model of
the exact final block objective, and then returns the long dilated sequence.
"""

import time
import math
import numpy as np

try:
    from scipy.optimize import minimize
    HAVE_SCIPY = True
except Exception:
    HAVE_SCIPY = False

_TARGET = 1.5027
_MICRO_M = 256
_FINAL_R = 27
_FINAL_N = _MICRO_M * _FINAL_R

CUR_BETA = 20.0

_CACHE_X = None
_CACHE_F = 0.0
_CACHE_G = None


def _score_key(item):
    return item[0]


def softmax(theta):
    z = np.asarray(theta, dtype=np.float64)
    z = z - np.max(z)
    e = np.exp(z)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.ones(z.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(z.size)
    return e / s


def _normalize_positive(x):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    x = np.maximum(x, 0.0)
    s = float(x.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return None
    return x / s


def _final_value_arrays(c, R):
    C = len(c)
    c_q = c[:, None]
    c_qm1 = np.empty((C, 1), dtype=np.float64)
    c_qm1[0, 0] = 0.0
    if C > 1:
        c_qm1[1:, 0] = c[:-1]

    sf = np.arange(R, dtype=np.float64)[None, :]
    coeff_q = sf + 1.0
    coeff_qm1 = (float(R) - 1.0) - sf

    vals = c_q * coeff_q + c_qm1 * coeff_qm1
    # The final fine lag corresponding to q = last macro lag and s = R-1
    # does not exist.
    vals[-1, -1] = -1.0e100
    return vals, coeff_q, coeff_qm1


def exact_final_score(x):
    """Exact evaluator score for the block-dilated sequence built from x."""
    x = _normalize_positive(x)
    if x is None:
        return float("inf")

    m = x.size
    R = _FINAL_R
    c = np.convolve(x, x, mode="full")
    vals, _, _ = _final_value_arrays(c, R)
    amax = float(vals.max())
    if not np.isfinite(amax):
        return float("inf")
    return 2.0 * float(m) * amax / float(R)


def smooth_final_F_grad(x, beta):
    """
    Smooth surrogate for the final block objective and Euclidean gradient
    on the simplex of block masses.
    """
    x = _normalize_positive(x)
    if x is None:
        return float("inf"), np.zeros(_MICRO_M, dtype=np.float64)

    m = x.size
    R = _FINAL_R
    c = np.convolve(x, x, mode="full")
    vals, coeff_q, coeff_qm1 = _final_value_arrays(c, R)

    b = float(beta) * vals
    bmax = float(b.max())
    e = np.exp(b - bmax)
    ssum = float(e.sum())
    if ssum <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(ssum):
        return float("inf"), np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)

    w = e / ssum
    F = (2.0 * float(m) / float(R)) * (bmax + math.log(ssum)) / float(beta)

    eff_q = np.sum(w * coeff_q, axis=1)
    eff_qm1 = np.sum(w * coeff_qm1, axis=1)
    eff = eff_q.copy()
    if eff.size > 1:
        eff[:-1] += eff_qm1[1:]

    # t_i = sum_k eff[k] x[k-i], with k-i in [0, m-1]
    conv_eff = np.convolve(x, eff[::-1], mode="full")
    t = conv_eff[2 * m - 2:m - 2:-1]
    grad = (4.0 * float(m) / float(R)) * t
    return F, grad


def _compute_theta_cached(theta):
    global _CACHE_X, _CACHE_F, _CACHE_G
    theta = np.asarray(theta, dtype=np.float64)
    if _CACHE_X is None or not np.array_equal(theta, _CACHE_X):
        x = softmax(theta)
        F, G = smooth_final_F_grad(x, CUR_BETA)
        _CACHE_X = theta.copy()
        _CACHE_F = F
        _CACHE_G = G
    return _CACHE_F, _CACHE_G


def theta_smooth_fun(theta):
    F, G = _compute_theta_cached(theta)
    return F


def theta_smooth_jac(theta):
    F, G = _compute_theta_cached(theta)
    return G


def optimize_smooth(x0, beta_schedule, maxiter):
    global CUR_BETA, _CACHE_X

    x = _normalize_positive(x0)
    if x is None:
        return None, float("inf")

    best = x.copy()
    best_score = exact_final_score(best)
    if not np.isfinite(best_score):
        return best, float("inf")

    theta = np.log(np.maximum(x, 1.0e-12))
    theta = theta - float(np.mean(theta))

    if not HAVE_SCIPY:
        return best, best_score

    for beta in beta_schedule:
        if time.monotonic() > 0 and False:
            pass
        CUR_BETA = float(beta)
        _CACHE_X = None

        options = {
            "maxiter": int(maxiter),
            "maxls": 50,
            "ftol": 1.0e-12,
            "gtol": 1.0e-6,
        }
        try:
            res = minimize(theta_smooth_fun, theta, jac=theta_smooth_jac,
                           method="L-BFGS-B", options=options)
            if res.x is not None:
                new_theta = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
                if np.all(np.isfinite(new_theta)):
                    theta = new_theta - float(np.mean(new_theta))
        except Exception:
            break

        x = softmax(theta)
        sc = exact_final_score(x)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best = x.copy()
            best_score = sc

    return best, best_score


def _safe_nonnegative_normalize(arr):
    x = np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64)
    if x.size == 0:
        return None
    minv = float(np.min(x))
    if minv < 1.0e-7:
        osc = x - 1.0
        if minv < 0.0:
            scale = 0.95 / max(1.0e-12, -minv)
            if scale < 1.0:
                x = 1.0 + scale * osc
        x = np.maximum(x, 1.0e-7)
    return _normalize_positive(x)


def uniform_start(m):
    return np.ones(m, dtype=np.float64) / float(m)


def sine_start(m, amp, phase):
    t = np.arange(m, dtype=np.float64) / float(m)
    f = 1.0 + float(amp) * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * t + float(phase))
    return _safe_nonnegative_normalize(f)


def harmonic_start(m, a1, ph1, a2, ph2):
    t = np.arange(m, dtype=np.float64) / float(m)
    f = 1.0 + float(a1) * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * t + float(ph1)) \
        + float(a2) * np.sin(4.0 * np.pi * t + float(ph2))
    return _safe_nonnegative_normalize(f)


def two_step_start(m, p, r):
    x = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
    k = int(round(float(p) * float(m)))
    k = max(1, min(m - 1, k))
    x[:k] = float(r)
    x[k:] = 1.0
    return _normalize_positive(x)


def exponential_start(m, lam, reverse=False):
    t = np.arange(m, dtype=np.float64) / float(m)
    f = np.exp(-float(lam) * t)
    if reverse:
        f = f[::-1]
    return _normalize_positive(f)


def trig_random_start(m, rng):
    t = np.arange(m, dtype=np.float64) / float(m)
    z = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
    R = 8
    for r in range(1, R + 1):
        amp = float(rng.normal(0.0, 1.0 / float(r + 1)))
        ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
        z += amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * float(r) * t + ph)
    z += float(rng.normal(0.0, 0.7)) * (2.0 * t - 1.0)
    f = 1.0 + 0.8 * np.tanh(z)
    return _normalize_positive(f)


def downsample_start(hs, m):
    try:
        arr = np.asarray(hs, dtype=np.float64)
    except Exception:
        return None
    arr = np.nan_to_num(arr, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
    if arr.size == 0:
        return None
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if s <= 0.0:
        return None
    arr = arr / s
    if arr.size == m:
        return arr.copy()
    xx = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, arr.size)
    tt = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m)
    out = np.interp(tt, xx, arr)
    return _normalize_positive(out)


def make_start_list(m, rng):
    starts = []

    u = uniform_start(m)
    if u is not None:
        starts.append(u)

    for amp in (0.35, 0.55, 0.70, 0.85, 0.95):
        for ph in (0.0, 0.5 * np.pi, np.pi, 1.5 * np.pi):
            s = sine_start(m, amp, ph)
            if s is not None:
                starts.append(s)

    for a1 in (0.65, 0.75, 0.85, 0.95):
        for a2 in (0.15, 0.30, 0.45):
            for ph2 in (0.0, np.pi):
                s = harmonic_start(m, a1, 0.5 * np.pi, a2, ph2)
                if s is not None:
                    starts.append(s)

    for p in (0.35, 0.45, 0.50):
        for r in (1.5, 2.0, 2.5):
            s = two_step_start(m, p, r)
            if s is not None:
                starts.append(s)

    for lam in (0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 3.0, 5.0):
        s = exponential_start(m, lam, reverse=False)
        if s is not None:
            starts.append(s)
        s = exponential_start(m, lam, reverse=True)
        if s is not None:
            starts.append(s)

    for _ in range(40):
        s = trig_random_start(m, rng)
        if s is not None:
            starts.append(s)

    try:
        d = downsample_start(height_sequence_1, m)
    except Exception:
        d = None
    if d is not None:
        starts.append(d)

    scored = []
    for x in starts:
        sc = exact_final_score(x)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            scored.append((sc, x.copy()))

    scored.sort(key=_score_key)
    return scored


def perturb_x(x, rng, noise):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    m = x.size
    u = float(rng.random())

    if u < 0.45:
        out = x.copy()
        L = int(rng.integers(1, max(2, m // 4 + 1)))
        st = int(rng.integers(0, m - L + 1))
        out[st:st + L] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, float(noise), L))
    elif u < 0.70:
        out = x.copy()
        out *= np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, max(1.0e-8, float(noise) * 0.3), m))
    elif u < 0.90:
        out = x.copy()
        t = np.arange(m, dtype=np.float64) / float(m)
        z = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
        R = 8
        for r in range(1, R + 1):
            amp = float(rng.normal(0.0, max(1.0e-8, float(noise) / float(r + 1))))
            ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
            z += amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * float(r) * t + ph)
        out *= np.exp(z)
    else:
        alpha = np.maximum(x, 1.0e-6) + 0.1
        x2 = rng.dirichlet(alpha)
        mix = min(0.5, max(0.05, float(noise)))
        out = (1.0 - mix) * x + mix * x2

    out = np.maximum(out, 0.0)
    s = float(out.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return x.copy()
    return out / s


def update_elite(elite, x, sc, max_len=6):
    if x is None or not np.isfinite(sc):
        return elite
    for ex_sc, ex_x in elite:
        if np.linalg.norm(np.asarray(x) - np.asarray(ex_x)) < 1.0e-5:
            if sc < ex_sc - 1.0e-12:
                elite = [(sc, np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).copy())]
                break
            return elite
    else:
        elite.append((float(sc), np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).copy()))

    elite.sort(key=_score_key)
    return elite[:max_len]


def basin_hopping(x0, rng, time_limit, deadline):
    best = np.asarray(x0, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    best = _normalize_positive(best)
    if best is None:
        best = uniform_start(_MICRO_M)
    best_score = exact_final_score(best)
    if not np.isfinite(best_score):
        best = uniform_start(_MICRO_M)
        best_score = exact_final_score(best)

    elite = update_elite([], best, best_score, 6)
    start = time.monotonic()
    noise = 0.15
    no_improve = 0

    while time.monotonic() < start + time_limit and time.monotonic() < deadline - 25.0:
        if len(elite) > 1 and float(rng.random()) < 0.35:
            parent = elite[int(rng.integers(0, len(elite)))]
            parent_x = parent[1]
        else:
            parent_x = best

        cand = perturb_x(parent_x, rng, noise)
        cand_score = exact_final_score(cand)

        if not HAVE_SCIPY:
            if cand_score < best_score:
                best = cand.copy()
                best_score = cand_score
                elite = update_elite(elite, best, best_score, 6)
                no_improve = 0
                noise = max(0.02, noise * 0.95)
            else:
                no_improve += 1
        else:
            if cand_score <= best_score + 0.05:
                if time.monotonic() < deadline - 30.0:
                    pol, pol_score = optimize_smooth(cand, (100.0, 300.0), 40)
                    if pol is not None and np.isfinite(pol_score) and pol_score < best_score:
                        best = pol.copy()
                        best_score = pol_score
                        elite = update_elite(elite, best, best_score, 6)
                        no_improve = 0
                        noise = max(0.02, noise * 0.95)
                    else:
                        no_improve += 1
            else:
                no_improve += 1

        if no_improve > 35:
            noise = min(0.60, noise * 1.30)
            no_improve = 0

        if best_score < 1.45:
            break

    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 30.0:
        pol, pol_score = optimize_smooth(best, (200.0, 600.0), 80)
        if pol is not None and np.isfinite(pol_score) and pol_score < best_score:
            best = pol.copy()
            best_score = pol_score

    return best, best_score


def propose_candidate():
    global CUR_BETA

    start_wall = time.monotonic()
    deadline = start_wall + 1790.0
    m = _MICRO_M
    rng = np.random.default_rng(20240611)

    scored = make_start_list(m, rng)
    if not scored:
        best = uniform_start(m)
        best_score = exact_final_score(best)
    else:
        best = scored[0][1].copy()
        best_score = float(scored[0][0])
        print("Initial best block score:", best_score)

    ideal_betas = (5.0, 20.0, 80.0, 300.0, 800.0)
    top = scored[:28]

    for idx, (sc, x) in enumerate(top):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 550.0:
            break

        if idx < 5:
            betas = ideal_betas
            maxit = 120
        elif idx < 14:
            betas = (20.0, 80.0, 300.0, 800.0)
            maxit = 90
        else:
            betas = (80.0, 300.0, 800.0)
            maxit = 70

        if time.monotonic() < deadline - 25:
            x_ref, sc_ref = optimize_smooth(x, betas, maxit)
            if x_ref is not None and np.isfinite(sc_ref) and sc_ref < best_score:
                best = x_ref.copy()
                best_score = float(sc_ref)
                print("Improved at start {}:".format(idx), best_score)

        if idx % 4 == 0 and time.monotonic() < deadline - 500.0:
            x_ref, sc_ref = optimize_smooth(best, (200.0, 600.0, 1200.0), 80)
            if x_ref is not None and np.isfinite(sc_ref) and sc_ref < best_score:
                best = x_ref.copy()
                best_score = float(sc_ref)
                print("Global polish improved:", best_score)

    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 250.0:
        x_ref, sc_ref = optimize_smooth(best, (5.0, 20.0, 80.0, 300.0, 900.0, 1600.0), 120)
        if x_ref is not None and np.isfinite(sc_ref) and sc_ref < best_score:
            best = x_ref.copy()
            best_score = float(sc_ref)

    remaining = max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic())
    if remaining > 180.0:
        hop_limit = min(1250.0, remaining - 80.0)
        best, best_score = basin_hopping(best, rng, hop_limit, deadline)
        print("After basin hopping:", best_score)

    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 35.0:
        x_ref, sc_ref = optimize_smooth(best, (300.0, 900.0, 1800.0), 100)
        if x_ref is not None and np.isfinite(sc_ref) and sc_ref < best_score:
            best = x_ref.copy()
            best_score = float(sc_ref)

    best = _normalize_positive(best)
    if best is None:
        best = uniform_start(m)

    final = np.repeat(best, _FINAL_R) * float(m)
    final = np.nan_to_num(final, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    final = np.clip(final, 0.0, 1000.0)

    out = [float(v) for v in final]

    # Optional sanity check using the actual evaluator if it is available.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 8.0:
        try:
            ev = float(evaluate_sequence(out))
            if not np.isfinite(ev):
                m_out = [float(v * float(m)) for v in best]
                return m_out
        except Exception:
            pass

    return out
```
